COMPLETED CLEANUP / PRE-EDIT DISPOSITION LEDGER — host storage and ingestion

Scope: all 15 *.test.ts files under agent-host/store and ingestion; no *.check.ts files exist there. Index is independently audited in host-index.txt. This ledger was written before test edits; validation results are appended afterwards.

Read: repository AGENTS.md/README.md and root/daemon package.json; agent-host README/service contracts; relevant production owners; GUI design storage/ingestion/output sections, thread-index/lazy-boot A2/C, and Claude/Codex fixture provenance. No production behavior changes are authorized by these dispositions.

Required command V: node --import tsx --import ./scripts/test/assert-ok.mjs --import ./scripts/test/quiet-mock-timers.mjs --test --test-concurrency=2 <path>. Scope baseline/final uses all store/*.test.ts and ingestion/*.test.ts. Parent owns pnpm check, pnpm test, final diff/commit/remote integration/push.

For every entry below, B1 names the independent contract; B2 names the concrete failure the case detects (the opposite of the titled behavior); B3 identifies its independent oracle; B4 its stable seam; B5 its refactor tolerance; B6 its ownership/unique scenario. The line numbers are pre-edit anchors. Parameterized declaration entries apply to every table row; explicit duplicate-row deletes follow.

Isolated-unit failure analysis before retention: attachment helpers can admit escaping/foreign/reserved IDs or misread legacy paths; binding merge can clear omitted identity, fail explicit clears or store invalid defaults; raw logs can leak secrets, lose later writes after a malformed frame or grow without bound; byte readers can count UTF-16 instead of bytes, accept stale cursors, include fragments or corrupt ordering after failed writes; snapshot saves can race deletion/mutation or trust an impossible cursor; output joins can mix calls, split Unicode, duplicate chunks or read uncommitted/stale bytes; ingestion can misclassify runtime events, lose/duplicate text, stamp the wrong owner/turn, confuse historical/live state or block a lifecycle transition. Retained cases target these failures, not declaration inventories.
